| 1 | SYNTH-0003 | Ashley Rodriguez (Association for Molecular Pathology) (executive director, Colorado) | HHS should at minimum require prospective outcome tracking for any AI tool deployed in clinical or coverage decision contexts before offering any regulatory or reimbursement incentives for adoption. |
| 2 | SYNTH-0005 | Charles Nelson (American Pharmacists Association) (research director, Iowa) | What would actually serve patients is rigorous post-market surveilance requirements, mandatory disclosure to patients when AI is involved in a clinical or coverage decision, and clear rules about who is legally liable when an AI-influenced decision causes harm. |
